([Heretek-AI/heretek-claude-harness](https://github.com/Heretek-AI/heretek-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/plugins/re-ai/tools/Android-RE/skills/android-re-network-intercept))
- **android-re-repackage**: Use when the user wants to modify an APK and produce a new signed build.
